# Colombia Wage Type Catalogue (WTC) - CO-WTC-001 v1.9

All six sheets of the delivered Colombia WTC: Version Control, the 405-row Wage Type Catalogue with every subjection column (withholding, health, solidarity fund, occupational risk, payroll levies, pension, salary class, benefit base, electronic-payroll element and legal basis), Employer Sick Pay, Tax Credit, Global WTC Coverage and Form Reference Guide.


## Provenance

| Source artifact | Bytes | MD5 |
|---|---|---|
| `CO-WTC-001-v1.9.xlsx` | 297,992 | `374f4545d144fd2d424f73604aa03e1a` |

This file is a complete, verbatim conversion of the artifacts above (python-docx / openpyxl, cached cell values; empty columns dropped; the first column `Row` of each table is the Excel row number, so a citation such as "WTC row 156, column Q" can be checked against the workbook). Figures, dates, codes, Spanish terminology and legal citations are reproduced exactly as delivered; nothing is summarised or rounded. The only change: the name of the client whose wage-type labels appear in a few rows is replaced by `[client]`.


## Reading notes (added at compilation, 2026-11-02)

- **Columns of the Wage Type Catalogue sheet** (header row 1). The subjection questions do not move together in Colombia, so
  each is answered separately: *Subject to Tax (Retención en la Fuente)*, *Table Tax (ET art. 383)*, *Special Tax (non-resident
  flat 20%, ET art. 408)*; *Subject to Health / Solidarity Pension Fund / Occupational Risk (ARL) / Payroll Levies (CCF-SENA-ICBF)
  / Pension*; *Salary or Non-Salary (CST arts. 127/128)*; statutory-benefit base; electronic-payroll element; legal basis.
- **`Yes/No`** in a subjection column means the answer depends on the condition in the row. For the class
  `Non-Salary - pacted` it means: out of the IBC, except the part above 40% of total remuneration (Law 1393 of 2010 art. 30),
  which enters health, pension and occupational risk only, never the payroll levies. `Non-Salary - non-remunerative` rows are
  outside the 40% test and outside the IBC. Where the nature of a payment is unclear the row is `pacted` and the DD non-salary
  pact flag decides (header of column V).
- **Column Y (electronic-payroll element)** of the pacted allowance rows (e.g. education allowances, rows 255-264): AuxilioNS
  (NIE142) where the payment is non-salary under the pact, otherwise AuxilioS (NIE141).
- **Severance savings and their interest** (rows 120-122, 321-322): no withholding at source for any employee, resident or
  non-resident (Law 100 of 1993 art. 135 par. 3; DUR 1625 art. 1.2.4.1.11); the 20% non-resident withholding is only a
  configurable conservative option.
- The *Mercans Code* column is empty in the whole sheet: the `CO-` codes are mnemonic and no numeric range has been assigned to
  Colombia yet. Do not state a numeric code for a `CO-` line.
- Sheet *Employer Sick Pay* states, as the CCG v1.7 does, two thirds for incapacity days 1-2 (Labour Code art. 227; Concepto
  MinSalud 202311602174531 of 20 October 2023; default 66.67%, configurable) and the 1-SMMLV floor (Constitutional Court C-543
  of 2007; applied, in proportion to the days). The incapacity / leave authorisation fields of the PILA (T2R2-057 to 060) are blank / zero
  under AT2 v30; the recovery from the EPS runs outside the PILA.
- Sheet *Tax Credit*: the 72-UVT dependant deduction of ET art. 336 num. 3 belongs to the annual return only; the 40% / 1,340 UVT
  cap applies in the monthly withholding (Decree 1625 of 2016 art. 1.2.4.1.6 par. 3). The 790, 1,340 and 3,800 UVT caps are
  annual and tracked year-to-date per employee, with no monthly one-twelfth limit (Consejo de Estado S4 rad. 24047 of 2021).

| Row | Mercans Code | Wage Type Name in English | Wage Type Name in Local Language (Spanish) | Wage Type Explanation | Category | Formula | Tax Code | Calculation Type | Prorating Method | Occurrence | Ignore If 0 | Enforce Setting | In Period Reconciliation | Subject to Tax (Retencion en la Fuente) | Table Tax (ET art. 383 progressive table 0/19/28/33/35/37/39%) | Special Tax (Non-resident flat 20% on gross, ET art. 408) | Subject to Health - Salud EPS (EE 4% + ER 8.5%; IBC, cap 25 SMMLV 43,772,625) | Subject to Solidarity Pension Fund - FSP (EE 1% + 0.2-1.0%; IBC >= 4 SMMLV 7,003,620) | Subject to Occupational Risk - ARL (ER only 0.348-8.7%; IBC) | Subject to Payroll Levies - CCF 4% / SENA 2% / ICBF 3% (Ley 21/1982 base, NO ceiling) | Subject to Pension - Pension Obligatoria (EE 4% + ER 12%; IBC, cap 25 SMMLV) | Salary or Non-Salary (CST arts. 127 / 128) - hard flag for the nomina electronica S / NS elements. Non-Salary is split for the Ley 1393 de 2010 art. 30 40% clamp: 'Non-Salary - pacted' = a payment that would remunerate the service but was excluded from salary by an express CST art. 128 pact (or a collective agreement) - INSIDE the 40% test; 'Non-Salary - non-remunerative' = non-salary by law or by nature (does not remunerate the service: transport allowance, occasional per diem, means and tools to do the job, dotacion, payments by mere liberality, indemnities, social-security benefits) - OUTSIDE the 40% test on both sides: not in the numerator (Consejo de Estado, Seccion Cuarta, sentencia de unificacion rad. 05001-23-33-000-2016-02496-01 (25185), 9 December 2021, rule 3) and not in the total remuneration (UGPP, ABC Empleadores 2025: total remunerado = all salary payments + pacted non-salary payments + paid leave). Where the nature is unclear the row is 'pacted' and the DD non-salary pact flag decides. 'Non-Salary - non-remunerative' maps to the nomina electronica NS element; 'Non-Salary - pacted' maps to the NS element where the non-salary pact holds (DD pact flag set) and, without the pact, to the S element (salary under CST art. 127). | Statutory-Benefit Base (prima de servicios / cesantias / intereses) | Vacation Base (CST art. 192 - excludes overtime and rest-day work only) | Nomina Electronica Element (Res. | 123 |  | Long Term Disability | (codigo global - ver la explicacion para el tratamiento colombiano) | Payroll levies = No: Law 21 of 1982 art. 17 limits the levy base to salary elements and paid rest days (Consejo de Estado, Seccion Cuarta, SU 25185 of 2021). The incapacity benefit is not salary (CST arts. 128 and 227) and is excluded from the SENA / ICBF / CCF base (Consejo de Estado, Seccion Cuarta, 19 August 2021, exp. 23856); an employer-paid top-up of that benefit has the same nature and is not paid rest. UGPP ABC Empleadores 2025 (p. 7) tells employers to take incapacity and maternity values into account for payroll levies, while its own table on p. 6 lists these events only for social security; the guide has no normative force and the Consejo de Estado ruling is followed here. Configurable. | Earning | Employer-paid disability top-up beyond the certified EPS or pension-administrator benefit | CO-TX-01 |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | No | No | Yes | Non-Salary - non-remunerative | No | No | Devengados / Incapacidad - Tipo (NIE126), Pago (NIE127) | Estatuto Tributario (Decreto-Ley 624 de 1989) arts. 383 (table as replaced by Ley 2010 de 2019 art. 42), 385, 386, 387, 388; Decreto 1625 de 2016 art. 1.2.4.1.6 par. 3 (as modified by Decreto 2231 de 2023 art. 9); Resolucion DIAN 000238 de 15 diciembre 2025 art. 1 (UVT 52,374); Certified EPS / AFP benefit taxable (DIAN Oficio 27408 de 2019 items 7.1, 7.4). Decreto 1072 de 2015 art. 2.2.4.2.1.6 (novedad 1.2): no ARL contribution for incapacity days. |  |  |

| 201 |  | Car allowance | (codigo global - ver la explicacion para el tratamiento colombiano) |  | Earning | Cash car / fuel / maintenance allowance per contract | CO-TX-01 |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes/No | Yes/No | Yes/No | No | Yes/No | Non-Salary - pacted | No | No | Devengados / Auxilio (AuxilioNS, NIE142, where non-salary under the pact; otherwise AuxilioS, NIE141) | Estatuto Tributario (Decreto-Ley 624 de 1989) arts. 383 (table as replaced by Ley 2010 de 2019 art. 42), 385, 386, 387, 388; Decreto 1625 de 2016 art. 1.2.4.1.6 par. 3 (as modified by Decreto 2231 de 2023 art. 9); Resolucion DIAN 000238 de 15 diciembre 2025 art. 1 (UVT 52,374). |  | Default 'Non-Salary - pacted'. The test is the purpose of the payment, not its name or a pact (Corte Suprema de Justicia SL5159-2018): a payment that remunerates the service is salary (CST art. 127) whatever the pact says; a benefit that does not remunerate the service and is excluded by a written CST art. 128 pact stays 'pacted', inside the Ley 1393 40% test. Where the amount is paid only so that the employee can travel for the job (means of transport, CST art. 128), configure it as 'Non-Salary - non-remunerative' with health, solidarity fund, occupational risk and pension = No: outside the contribution base and the 40% test (Consejo de Estado, Seccion Cuarta, SU 25185 of 9 December 2021). |

## Sheet: Employer Sick Pay

COLOMBIA - EMPLOYER SICK PAY AND THE INCAPACITY CASCADE (2026)  
Colombia has NO employer sick-pay scheme of the Czech kind. Cash sickness benefit is a social-security  
benefit, and the employer bears only the FIRST TWO DAYS of a general-illness incapacity.  
WHO PAYS WHAT  
General illness, days 1-2 .......... EMPLOYER 66.67% (Mercans default, configurable)  
General illness, days 3-90 ......... EPS (health insurer) 66.67% of the contribution base  
General illness, days 91-180 ....... EPS 50.00% of the contribution base  
General illness, days 181-540 ...... AFP (pension admin.) 50.00% - 'subsidio equivalente' (Ley 100 art. 41);  
the EPS pays if no rehabilitation concept was issued before day 120  
General illness, day 541 onward .... EPS (ADRES-funded) amount recognised by the EPS (Decreto 780 art. 2.2.3.4.3)  
Occupational accident or disease ... ARL (risk insurer) 100.00% of the contribution base,  
from the DAY AFTER the event  
Maternity leave, 18 weeks .......... EPS salary earned when the leave begins  
Paternity leave, 2 weeks ........... EPS full salary (expands 1 week per point of structural  
unemployment reduction, capped at 5 weeks)  
FORMULAE  
Employer days 1-2 ............. S / 30 x 2/3 x d, floored at the minimum-wage equivalent  
EPS days 3-90 ................. IBC / 30 x 2/3 x d, floored at the minimum-wage equivalent  
EPS days 91-180 ............... IBC / 30 x 0.5000 x d, floored at the minimum-wage equivalent  
AFP days 181-540 .............. IBC / 30 x 0.5000 x d; IBC of the month before the INITIAL incapacity  
ARL, occupational ............. IBC / 30 x 1.0000 x d  
Maternity ..................... S / 30 x 126 calendar days  
Paternity ..................... S / 30 x 14 calendar days  
Variable salary ............... average of the year of service preceding the incapacity, or of the  
whole period if under a year (CST art. 228)  
THE RATE FOR THE EMPLOYER'S FIRST TWO DAYS.  
Decreto 2943 de 2013 art. 1, compiled at Decreto 780 de 2016 art. 3.2.1.10, allocates 'las  
prestaciones economicas correspondientes a los dos (2) primeros dias de incapacidad originada por  
enfermedad general' to the EMPLOYER but does NOT state the rate. CST art. 227 sets the auxilio at two  
thirds for the first 90 days, and Concepto MinSalud 202311602174531 of 20 October 2023 applies it to  
days 1-2 (66.6% of salary); a minority practice pays 100%. MERCANS DEFAULT: 66.67%, configurable.  
THE MINIMUM-WAGE FLOOR ON INCAPACITY PAYMENTS.  
Where 66.67% of salary falls below one SMMLV (1,750,905), the payment is topped up to one  
minimum wage, in proportion to the days: Corte Constitucional C-543 de 2007  
holds that the sickness benefit may never fall below the minimum legal wage. MERCANS DEFAULT: apply  
the floor.  
PAYROLL TREATMENT (the counter-intuitive part)  
Employer days 1-2 ....... NOT salary, but IN the IBC; NOT in the Ley 21/1982 payroll-levy base (art. 17) and NOT in  
the statutory-benefit base (the days count, the amount does not); TAXABLE.  
EPS / ARL benefits ...... NOT employer salary, so OUT of the payroll-levy base - but CONTRIBUTIONS  
CONTINUE ON THE BASE (PILA change events IGE, IRL, LMA). TAXABLE -  
not exempt income: the benefits are not indemnities under ET art. 206  
nums. 1-2 (DIAN Oficio 27408 de 2019 items 7.1-7.4). Advanced by the employer  
through payroll and recovered from the insurer outside the PILA: under AT2 v30 the  
authorisation number and value (T2R2-057 to T2R2-060) are reported blank / zero.  
Auxilio de transporte ... NOT PAYABLE for sick days - no travel occurs.  
Overtime and premia ..... Not applicable during incapacity. No ARL contribution for incapacity days (Decreto 1072 art. 2.2.4.2.1.6).  
Benefit accrual ......... Continues: incapacity days count for prima, cesantias and vacation, but the  
incapacity amount is left out of the salary average (CSJ SL1659-2025); SUSPENDED only for unpaid leave (CST art. 53).  
Sources: CST arts. 227, 228, 236, 239; Corte Constitucional C-543 de 2007; Concepto MinSalud 202311602174531 de 2023; Decreto 2943 de 2013 art. 1; Decreto 780 de 2016 art. 3.2.1.10;  
ET art. 206 nums. 1-2 and par. 1; DIAN Oficio 27408 de 2019; Ley 100 de 1993 art. 41; Ley 1753 de 2015 art. 67; Decreto 2126 de 2023; Decreto 1072 de 2015 art. 2.2.4.2.1.6; Ley 361 de 1997 art. 26; Resolucion MSPS 2388 de 2016 Anexo Tecnico 2;  
Resolucion DIAN 000227 de 2025 arts. 1.5.3.1.1 to 1.5.3.9.8, Anexo Tecnico v1.0.  

## Sheet: Tax Credit

COLOMBIA - TAX CREDITS: NOT APPLICABLE. THE EQUIVALENT MECHANISMS ARE DEDUCTIONS AND EXEMPT INCOME.  
Colombia has NO tax credit against the payroll withholding - no personal credit, no child credit, no  
disability credit and no non-refundable family credit of the kind the CZ template contemplates. Relief  
is delivered instead by SUBTRACTING amounts from the base, in THREE legally distinct classes whose  
class determines where each item sits in the ET art. 388 depuration and which caps it consumes.  
Getting the class wrong is the most common source of withholding error in Colombia.  
UVT (tax unit) 2026 = COP 52,374 (Resolucion DIAN 000238 de 15 diciembre 2025 art. 1; the 2026  
uprating was certified at 5.17%, taking the unit from COP 49,799). Every tax parameter is indexed to  
the UVT and NO tax threshold is expressed in minimum wages - that is the one-caliber discipline:  
minimum wage for social security and labour, UVT for tax. The two are never mixed.  
CLASS A - NON-TAXABLE INCOME (ingresos no constitutivos de renta ni ganancia ocasional)  
Subtracted at step (B), BEFORE the 40% cap reference figure is struck, so it does NOT consume the cap.  
A1 Mandatory pension contribution ......... 4% of the IBC .................... ET art. 55  
A2 Solidarity pension fund ............... 1% + 0.2-1.0% of the IBC .......... ET art. 55  
A3 Mandatory health contribution ......... 4% of the IBC .................... ET art. 56  
A4 Voluntary contributions to the individual pension account (RAIS):  
the lower of 25% of annual labour income and 2,500 UVT = COP 130,935,000 . ET art. 55  
(a non-qualifying withdrawal is withheld at 35%)  
A5 Third-party meal payments: not income up to 41 UVT/month = 2,147,334,  
where the employee's salary does not exceed 310 UVT = 16,235,940 ..... ET art. 387-1  
CLASS B - DEDUCTIONS AND EXEMPT INCOME (deducciones y rentas exentas)  
Subtracted at steps (D) and (E), INSIDE the 40% and 1,340-UVT caps.  
B1 Housing-loan interest and monetary correction ... 100 UVT/month = COP 5,237,400 . ET arts. 119, 387  
B2 Prepaid medicine / health insurance ............ 16 UVT/month = COP 837,984 . ET art. 387 lits. a, b  
(the provider must be supervised by the National Health Superintendence or the  
Financial Superintendence)  
B3 Dependants: 10% of the month's gross labour income, capped 32 UVT = COP 1,675,968 . ET art. 387  
NOTE: 10% OF GROSS REGARDLESS OF THE NUMBER OF DEPENDANTS - it does not multiply.  
B4 Voluntary pension funds + AFC / AVC long-term savings: the lower of 30% of labour  
income and 3,800 UVT/year = COP 199,021,200, SHARED between the two vehicles; annual cap tracked year-to-date, no monthly one-twelfth limit . ET arts. 126-1, 126-4; DUR 1625 de 2016 art. 1.2.1.22.43  
B5 THE 25% EXEMPT-INCOME DEDUCTION ................ 790 UVT/year = COP 41,375,460 . ET art. 206 num. 10  
COMPUTED LAST, on the figure net of non-taxable income, the art. 387 deductions and all other  
exempt income: 'El calculo de esta renta exenta se efectuara una vez se detraiga del valor  
total de los pagos laborales recibidos por el trabajador, los ingresos no constitutivos de  
renta, las deducciones y las demas rentas exentas diferentes a la establecida en el presente  
numeral.' Computing 25% of GROSS overstates it and under-withholds.  
Annual cap, tracked year-to-date: each month min(25% of the month's figure, 790 UVT - exemption taken YTD).  
B6 Cesantias and their interest: exempt in full where the average monthly income of the last six  
months is <= 350 UVT = 18,330,900; then tapering 90 / 80 / 60 / 40 / 20 / 0%  
through 410 / 470 / 530 / 590 / 650 UVT ......................... ET art. 206 num. 4  
They are EXCLUDED from the monthly base entirely under both procedures, and the 25%  
exemption does NOT apply to them (art. 206 par. 2).  
B7 Pension income: taxable only above 1,000 UVT/month = COP 52,374,000 ...... ET art. 206 num. 5  
B8 Accident, illness and maternity-protection indemnities and burial expenses: exempt ONLY up to  
the statutory minimum amount ................................. ET art. 206 nums. 1-3, par. 1  
CLASS C - ANNUAL INCOME-TAX RETURN ONLY (outside the caps; not a withholding factor)  
C1 Additional dependants deduction: 72 UVT per dependant per year = COP 3,770,928 each,  
maximum 4 dependants = COP 15,083,712/year (no monthly apportionment: not applied in withholding)  
............................................... ET art. 336 num. 3, second paragraph  
'en adicion al limite establecido en el inciso anterior' - ANNUAL INCOME-TAX RETURN ONLY: it is NOT  
a withholding-base factor (DIAN Concepto 011383 de 2024, IV.2: art. 388 refers to art. 387, not 336).  
STACKING (annual return only): an EMPLOYEE may take BOTH B3 and C1 FOR THE SAME DEPENDANT. Decreto 2231 de 2023 art. 3:  
'el mismo dependiente solo dara lugar a una de estas dos deducciones, excepto cuando el  
contribuyente tenga ingresos por rentas de trabajo provenientes de una relacion laboral o  
legal y reglamentaria, en cuyo caso podran aplicarse ambas deducciones por el mismo  
dependiente.'  
THE COMBINED CAP  
(D + E) <= 40% of (C = A - B) AND (D + E) <= 1,340 UVT/year = COP 70,181,160  
Annual cap, tracked year-to-date: each month (D + E) <= min(40% of the month's (C), 1,340 UVT - amount taken YTD).  
NO MONTHLY ONE-TWELFTH LIMIT: the Consejo de Estado (Seccion Cuarta, rad. 24047, 28 October 2021) annulled the monthly  
equivalent ('es decir ... UVT mensuales') of Decreto 1625 de 2016 art. 1.2.4.1.6 par. 3; Decreto 2231 de 2023 art. 9 restated  
it in annual UVT only. DIAN Concepto 011383 de 2024 (IV.1): the agent observes the annual limit per individual.  
The CURRENT year's UVT applies to the annual caps (DIAN Concepto 015007 int. 973 de 13 septiembre 2023).  
NOT AVAILABLE AGAINST WITHHOLDING  
The 1% / 240-UVT invoice-based deduction of ET art. 336 num. 5 is expressly excluded - 'no se tendra  
en cuenta para el calculo de la retencion en la fuente' - confirmed by DIAN Concepto 015007 int. 973  
de 13 septiembre 2023. It is available only in the employee's own annual return.  
NOT A CREDIT, AND NOT A TAX  
The Contribucion Solidaria a la Educacion Superior (Contribucion Sabes) is a CST art. 150 permitted  
DEDUCTION added by Ley 1911 de 2018 art. 22, not a tax and not a credit - it must never be  
aggregated with the retencion en la fuente on Formulario 350.  
DECLARATIONS THE EMPLOYEE MUST MAKE (no relief is automatic except the 25%)  
Housing-loan interest ... lender certificate. Prepaid health ... provider certificate.  
Dependants .............. number, EACH DEPENDANT'S IDENTITY DOCUMENT, relationship and basis of  
dependency; for a dependent spouse, parent or sibling, a public  
accountant's certificate of annual income below 260 UVT (COP 13,617,240)  
or a certified state of physical or psychological dependence.  
Voluntary pension / AFC / AVC / RAIS ... fund or administrator certificates.  
Higher withholding rate ... written request, effective the month AFTER it is made (ET art. 383 par. 3).  
WHO QUALIFIES AS A DEPENDANT (ET art. 387 par. 2 nums. 2-3 as amended by Ley 2411 de 2024 art. 2):  
children up to 18; children 18-25 whose higher or technical education the taxpayer finances;  
children over 18 in a certified state of dependence; a spouse or permanent partner dependent by  
absence of income or with annual income below 260 UVT; parents and siblings on the same conditions.  
OUT OF MERCANS SCOPE BUT FLAGGED SO THE ENGINE DOES NOT SILENTLY CLAMP THEM  
ET art. 206 par. 4 and Decreto 1625 de 2016 art. 1.2.4.1.6 par. 3 place art. 206 nums. 6-9, the  
prima especial and the prima de costo de vida OUTSIDE the 40% and 1,340-UVT limits. These attach to  
military, judicial, diplomatic and public university staff - out of private-sector scope.  
NON-RESIDENTS GET NONE OF THE ABOVE  
ET art. 408: a flat 20% on the GROSS payment, with no depuration, no art. 387 deductions, no 25%  
exempt income and no tax-free band. DIAN Concepto 011108 de 29 junio 2026.  

## Sheet: Global WTC Coverage


| Row | # | Item | Detail |
|---|---|---|---|
| 2 | 1 | Global baseline file | shared/Global_WTC_V1_0.xlsx |
| 3 | 2 | Global codes in the baseline | 300 |
| 4 | 3 | Global codes included in the Colombia WTC | 300 - ALL of them. None excluded. Every code configured FRESH for Colombia 2026 against CO-CCG-001 Modules 3-7 and the primary law. |
| 5 | 4 | Global codes excluded | 0 |
| 6 | 5 | Colombia-specific additions | 102 CO-prefixed wage types: 55 earnings (core pay, the six premium hour types, the statutory benefits, the leave cascade, the termination heads), 19 employee deductions, 11 employer contributions and accruals, 17 bases / intermediaries / rates / flags. |
| 7 | 6 | Total rows in the Wage Type Catalogue | 402 |
| 8 | 7 | SS-fund column expansion (why five, not one) | Colombia has EIGHT mandatory contributions across THREE insurance branches, THREE payroll levies and ONE employee-only solidarity surcharge, and they do NOT share a base. Q1 = Salud (EE 4% + ER 8.5%, IBC, cap 25 SMMLV, ER exonerable). Q2 = Fondo de Solidaridad Pensional (EE only, 1% from 4 SMMLV plus 0.2-1.0% from 16 SMMLV, IBC). Q3 = Riesgos Laborales / ARL (ER only, 0.348-8.7% by work centre, IBC). Q4 = Parafiscales CCF 4% + SENA 2% + ICBF 3% on the LEY 21 DE 1982 ART. 17 BASE WITH NO CEILING. R = Pension (EE 4% + ER 12%, IBC, cap 25 SMMLV, never exonerable). Collapsing Q4 into the others would silently cap the levies at 25 SMMLV and would drop paid rest days from their base. |
| 9 | 8 | Three Colombia-specific base columns added (S, T, U) | S = the HARD CST art. 127 / 128 salary flag, which the nomina electronica demands at ELEMENT LEVEL through paired S / NS element names and which must never be derived at reporting time. T = the statutory-benefit base (prima / cesantias / intereses), which INCLUDES the auxilio de transporte even though the IBC and the levy base exclude it. U = the vacation base of CST art. 192, which excludes ONLY rest-day work and supplementary overtime hours, so the night premium and commissions stay in. Values: Salary / Non-Salary / Paid Rest / Conditional / NA for S; Yes / No / Yes-No / NA for T and U. |
| 10 | 9 | Column meaning adopted (N, O, P) | N = subject to retencion en la fuente at all. O = subject to the ET art. 383 progressive table (0 / 19 / 28 / 33 / 35 / 37 / 39% on the art. 388 depurated base). P = SPECIAL TAX is used for the NON-RESIDENT flat 20% on gross of ET art. 408 - the treatment that produces a 17-fold withholding difference on the same salary and is the single largest configuration risk in Colombian expatriate payroll. |
| 11 | 10 | Yes-No entries and their justification | Every Yes-No (rendered 'Yes/No') marks a genuine statutory conditionality, and column D carries the explanation in every case, as the v2.5 C3 guard requires. The recurring reasons are: (a) the LEY 1393/2010 ART. 30 40% CLAMP, which excludes a validly pacted non-salary payment from the IBC only up to 40% of total remuneration and forces the excess back in; (b) the ET art. 387-1 meal thresholds; (c) the ET art. 206 num. 4 cesantias taper; (d) the ET art. 206 par. 1 statutory-minimum limit on EPS / ARL benefit exemptions; (e) the apprentice's phase-dependent regime; (f) the CST art. 130 viaticos split; and (g) the conditional character of an anticipo. |
| 12 | 11 | 2026 parameters used, and their verification | SMMLV 1,750,905 (Decreto 1469 de 2025 art. 1 - VERIFIED in the source PDF, which also records that the Council of State suspended the decree on 12 February 2026, that Decreto 0159 de 2026 re-fixed the identical amount, and that the suspension was revoked on 9 July 2026, so the AMOUNT was never in dispute). UVT 52,374 (Resolucion DIAN 000238 de 15-12-2025 art. 1 - VERIFIED in the source PDF). Auxilio de transporte 249,095 (Decreto 1470 de 2025 art. 1 - the decree itself is not in 00-official-sources; the figure is carried from CO-CCG-001 and is corroborated by the exact COP 2,000,000 combined minimum cash entitlement). IBC ceiling 25 SMMLV = 43,772,625 and salario integral at 70% (Ley 797 de 2003 art. 5 - VERIFIED verbatim). 40% clamp (Ley 1393 de 2010 art. 30 - VERIFIED verbatim). Levy base including paid rest days (Ley 21 de 1982 art. 17 - VERIFIED verbatim). Rest-day premium 80% -> 90% on 1 July 2026 (Ley 2466 de 2025 art. 14 - VERIFIED verbatim). Night window 19:00-06:00 (Ley 2466 art. 10 - VERIFIED verbatim). 42-hour week (Ley 2466 art. 11 - VERIFIED verbatim). Salary in kind 50% / 30% (CST art. 129 - VERIFIED verbatim). Vacation base (CST art. 192 - VERIFIED verbatim). Viaticos (CST art. 130 - VERIFIED verbatim). Tips (CST art. 131 - VERIFIED verbatim). Dotacion every four months (CST arts. 230, 232 - VERIFIED verbatim). Apprentice 75% / 100% (Ley 2466 art. 21 - VERIFIED verbatim). Connectivity allowance (Ley 2466 art. 53 - VERIFIED verbatim). Cesantias 8.33% monthly advance (Ley 2466 art. 64 - VERIFIED verbatim). |
| 13 | 12 | Newest-law check (control C2) | LEY 2381 DE 2024, the complete replacement of the Colombian pension architecture, is the newest pension law on the statute book and it does NOT govern any 2026 payroll period. Its own art. 94 set entry into force at 1 July 2025; Constitutional Court Auto 841 de 18 junio 2025 SUSPENDED that entry into force; and Judgment C-264 de 25 agosto 2026 upheld art. 94 on the understanding that the upheld provisions take effect on 1 APRIL 2027. Every 2026 period therefore runs on Ley 100 de 1993 as amended by Ley 797 de 2003, and NO part of Ley 2381 is configured as live. What changes in 2027: only the solidarity-fund surcharge, which becomes a single five-band 1.5% / 1.8% / 2.5% / 2.8% / 3.0% scale - the 16% total, the 12 / 4 split and the 25-SMMLV ceiling are unchanged. Recorded as a 2027 change-control item. |
| 14 | 13 | Configuration source | Configured for Colombia from CO-CCG-001 Modules 3-10, the report specifications and the primary law (CST Decreto 2663 de 1950, Ley 100 de 1993, Ley 797 de 2003, Ley 1122 de 2007, Ley 1393 de 2010, Ley 21 de 1982, Ley 89 de 1988, Ley 2101 de 2021, Ley 2381 de 2024, Ley 2466 de 2025, Decreto 1469 de 2025, Decreto 0223 de 2026, Decreto 1990 de 2016, Decreto Ley 624 de 1989, Decreto Ley 2090 de 2003, Resoluciones DIAN 000227 y 000238 de 2025, Resolucion 2388 de 2016 y Anexo 2, Oficio DIAN 13840 de 2025). |
| 15 | 14 | DERIVED flags | (1) The Mercans code numbers for the 102 Colombia-specific rows are DERIVED - no Mercans numeric code range has been assigned for Colombia, so mnemonic CO- codes are used and must be renumbered at system configuration. (2) The auxilio de transporte amount (249,095) is fixed by Decreto 1470 de 2025 art. 1. (3) The additional high-risk pension rate is +10 points borne by the employer (26% total against 16% standard): Decreto-Ley 2090 de 2003 art. 5, matching the Resolucion 2388 rate table. |
| 16 | 15 | Open items and Mercans defaults | (1) The rate for the employer's first two sick days - closed: two thirds of salary (CST art. 227, applied to days 1-2 by Concepto MinSalud 202311602174531 of 20 October 2023; Decreto 780 de 2016 art. 3.2.1.10 allocates the days but not the rate); Mercans default 66.67%, configurable for a client that pays 100%. (2) The minimum-wage floor on incapacity payments - closed: Corte Constitucional C-543 de 2007 (the sickness benefit may not fall below the minimum legal wage); the floor is applied, in proportion to the days. (3) The additional employer pension contribution for Decreto Ley 2090 de 2003 high-risk activities - +10 points (art. 5), regime extended by Decreto 1435 de 2025 until Congress legislates; no rule found for workers who started a high-risk activity between 1 January 2025 and that decree. |
| 17 | 16 | Egypt F20 guards | COMMISSION IS PRESENT as a first-class wage type (CO-COMISIONES) with its own nomina electronica element, and the global commission-bearing codes are configured, not dropped. EVERY tax code in column G is drawn from the Colombian legend below (CO-TX-01 to CO-TX-10 and CO-NA) and the validator rejects any code that does not start with 'CO-'. NO Yes-No appears in a taxability or subjection column without an explanation in column D. |
| 19 | TAX CODE LEGEND (C9 - every code defined and sourced to a Colombian authority publication) |  |  |
| 20 | Tax Code | Meaning | Official Source |
| 21 | CO-TX-01 | Ordinary taxable work income - RESIDENT. Enters the total taxable payments of the month, is depurated under ET art. 388 (non-taxable income, art. 387 deductions, exempt income, the 40% cap and the 1,340-UVT annual cap) and is withheld on the ET art. 383 progressive table (0/19/28/33/35/37/39%) under Procedure 1 (art. 385) or at the fixed percentage under Procedure 2 (art. 386). No preferential rate and no tax-free allowance exists for any individual earning type. | Estatuto Tributario (Decreto-Ley 624 de 1989) arts. 383 (table as replaced by Ley 2010 de 2019 art. 42), 385, 386, 387, 388; Decreto 1625 de 2016 art. 1.2.4.1.6 par. 3 (as modified by Decreto 2231 de 2023 art. 9); Resolucion DIAN 000238 de 15 diciembre 2025 art. 1 (UVT 52,374). |
| 22 | CO-TX-02 | Severance savings and the interest on them (cesantias e intereses). EXCLUDED from the monthly withholding base under BOTH procedures - they never enter the monthly table read. Exempt IN FULL where the average monthly income of the last 6 months is <= 350 UVT (18,330,900); above that the untaxed portion tapers 90/80/60/40/20/0% through 650 UVT. The 25% exemption of art. 206 num. 10 does NOT apply to them (art. 206 par. 2). NOT WITHHELD for any employee, resident or non-resident (Ley 100 de 1993 art. 135 par. 3; DUR 1625 de 2016 art. 1.2.4.1.11): the ET art. 408 flat 20% does not apply to them; the conservative 20% for non-residents is a configurable option, off by default. | ET arts. 206 num. 4 and par. 2, 385, 386; Ley 100 de 1993 art. 135 par. 3 and DUR 1625 de 2016 art. 1.2.4.1.11 (no withholding; DIAN Oficios 8899 de 2015 and 4358 de 2019); Ley 50 de 1990 art. 99; Ley 52 de 1975 art. 1; Ley 2466 de 2025 art. 64 (8.33% monthly advance deposit). |
| 23 | CO-TX-03 | Statutory minimum service bonus (prima de servicios legal). Under Procedure 1 it is withheld SEPARATELY, on its own read of the ET art. 383 table, and is NOT added to the month's salary. Under Procedure 2 it is folded into the ordinary monthly base (the 13 divisor of art. 386 already prorates its effect). Any excess above the statutory minimum bonus goes to the ordinary monthly base. | ET arts. 385 and 386; CST arts. 306 (as amended by Ley 1788 de 2016 art. 1), 307 and 308. |
| 24 | CO-TX-04 | Non-taxable income - ingreso no constitutivo de renta ni ganancia ocasional. Subtracted at step (B) of the ET art. 388 depuration, i.e. BEFORE the 40% cap reference figure is struck, which makes it more valuable than exempt income. Covers the employee mandatory pension 4%, the solidarity pension fund 1% + 0.2-1.0%, the mandatory health 4%, and voluntary contributions to the individual pension account within 25% of annual labour income / 2,500 UVT. | ET arts. 55 and 56; ET art. 388 num. 1; Formato 2276 v4 numerals N23 and N24 (which group the pension and solidarity-fund contributions on a single line). |
| 25 | CO-TX-05 | Conditionally not income - third-party meal payments (pagos de alimentacion a terceros). NOT income to the employee where BOTH conditions hold: the employee's salary does not exceed 310 UVT (16,235,940) AND the payment is made to a THIRD PARTY. Where the month's payments exceed 41 UVT (2,147,334) the EXCESS is taxable employment income. Cash paid to the employee for food is salary and gets none of this treatment. | ET art. 387-1; Formato 2276 v4 numerals N08 (excess) and N32 (within-limit amount), which report the two portions separately; Formulario 220 casillas 37 and 38. |
| 26 | CO-TX-06 | Not income and not remuneration - amounts given not to enrich the employee but to enable the work: statutory work clothing (dotacion), work tools, protective equipment, means of transport, representation expenses, occasional viaticos for transport, and genuine documented business-expense reimbursement. Outside EVERY base - tax, contribution, payroll-levy, statutory-benefit and vacation. | CST arts. 128 and 230 (art. 230 as modified by Ley 11 de 1984 art. 7); CST arts. 130 and 232; ET art. 387-1 final paragraph (representation expenses expressly outside the meal rule). |
| 27 | CO-TX-07 | Exempt income LIMITED TO THE STATUTORY MINIMUM AMOUNT - accident and illness indemnities, maternity-protection indemnities and employee burial expenses. ET art. 206 par. 1 is explicit that the exemption 'operates only on the amounts corresponding to the statutory minimum' and that the excess is not exempt. It does NOT extend to incapacity or maternity / paternity benefits, which are taxable (DIAN Oficio 27408 de 2019 items 7.1-7.4). | ET art. 206 nums. 1, 2, 3 and par. 1; CST arts. 227, 236; Decreto 780 de 2016 art. 3.2.1.10 (Decreto 2943 de 2013 art. 1, employer days 1-2). |
| 28 | CO-TX-08 | NON-RESIDENT flat withholding - 20% ON THE GROSS PAYMENT, with NO art. 388 depuration, NO 25% exempt income, NO art. 387 deductions and NO tax-free band, for Colombian-source work income. Determined at the moment of EACH payment on the status then held; the switch to the art. 383 table is PROSPECTIVE from the payment following the 183rd day, never retroactive. Where the service is performed entirely outside Colombia the income is foreign-source and there is NO withholding. NOTE: this code is a RESIDENCY-DRIVEN OVERLAY, applied per employee through column P (Special Tax) rather than assigned to any specific wage-type row -- zero rows carrying this code in the main catalogue is expected, not an omission. | ET arts. 10, 408-415; DIAN Concepto 011108 de 29 junio 2026 (upholding Concepto 1789 int. 14595 de 2025); DIAN Oficio 28380 de 2019; Formulario 350 casillas 109-112 and hoja 2 casillas 141-155. |
| 29 | CO-TX-09 | Exempt income - renta exenta - for voluntary pension fund contributions (art. 126-1) and construction-savings / long-term-savings account contributions (arts. 126-4 AFC/AVC), employee or employer-paid on the employee's behalf. Subtracted at step (E) INSIDE the 40% and 1,340-UVT caps, within a single shared annual limit of the lower of 30% of labour income or 3,800 UVT (COP 199,021,200 for 2026). | ET arts. 126-1 and 126-4; ET art. 388 num. 2; Formulario 220 casillas 56, 57 and 58; Formato 2276 v4 numerals N26, N27 and N28. |
| 30 | CO-TX-10 | Pension income and refunds of pension savings. A retirement, invalidity, old-age, survivor or occupational-risk pension is taxable only on the monthly amount exceeding 1,000 UVT (COP 52,374,000). The 40% limit of art. 388 expressly does not apply to pension payments, pension substitution indemnities or refunds of pension savings. Paid by the pension administrator, so OUT OF SCOPE of employer gross-to-net; retained for completeness and for Formulario 220 casilla 50 / Formato 2276 numeral N21. | ET arts. 206 num. 5 and par. 3, 383 (first paragraph, which brings pensions into the same table), 388 final sentence; Formulario 220 casilla 50; Formato 2276 v4 numeral N21. |
| 31 | CO-NA | Not a remuneration line - an employee deduction, an employer contribution or accrual, a calculation base, an intermediary, a notional, a rate or a configuration flag. Every taxability and subjection column is NA because the row is a mechanism, not a payment to the employee. | N/A - mechanism line. Reported, where reported at all, in the Deducciones block of the nomina electronica (Resolucion DIAN 000227 de 2025 arts. 1.5.3.1.1 to 1.5.3.9.8, Anexo Tecnico v1.0) or in the PILA settlement record (Resolucion MSPS 2388 de 2016 Anexo Tecnico 2). |
